Let's derive q(s) for 0≤s≤2. Components:

q00(s) = (1/4) I0(s):
For 0<s≤1: π/4. For 1<s≤2: ∫_{s-1}^{1} 1/(4√{u(s-u)}). We derived I0 = 2[asin√(1/ρ?)?]. Let's get formula. Let s=ρ for ρ>1. Integral J=∫_{ρ-1}^{1} du/√{u(ρ-u)}. We can use J=2[arcsin√(1/ρ) - arcsin√((ρ-1)/ρ)]? Earlier with u=ρ sin²θ: lower θ1=arcsin√((ρ-1)/ρ), upper θ2=arcsin√(1/ρ). J=2(θ2-θ1). So q00(s)= (1/4)*J =0.5(θ2-θ1). At s=1: θ2=π/2 θ1=0 =>π/4. s=2: θ2=π/2 θ1=π/2 =>0. Good.
q_bb(s): uniform on [a,1] a=1-L, density 1/L. Convolution of two uniform length L shifted. Let r=s-2a = s-(2-2L). Support r∈[0,2L]. For r∈[0,L]: overlap length L-r; density 1/L² => q=(L-r)/L². For r∈[L,2L]: length 2L-r; q=(2L-r)/L². Max at r=0 (s=2-2L=2a) value 1/L; min endpoint.
q_pb(s): ∫ p0(u) b(s-u) du. By symmetry q_bp same. For given s, u such that u∈[0,1], y=s-u∈[a,1], and y≥0. Intersection: u∈[max(0,s-1), min(1, s-a)]? Since y≥a => u≤s-a; y≤1 => u≥s-1. Also u∈[0,1]. So interval [A,B] = [max(0,s-1), min(1,s-a)], if A≤B. q_pb(s)= (1/L)∫_A^B (1/(2√u)) du = (1/(2L))(2(√B-√A)) = (√B-√A)/L.
Thus exact. Nice.

Detailed _optimize_p(p, seconds, gamma_list, rng):
Input p np.array sum1 length m. It will loop over gamma from low to high (e.g. [5,20,100,300,1000]) with fractions. For each gamma, iteration until time.
Pseudo:

def _project_simplex(v):
  #.maximum? standard
  if v.size==0 return v
  u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
  css=np.cumsum(u)
  rho=np.nonzero(u>0 & (1+u*np.arange(1,n+1)>css))[0][-1]? Need avoid boolean indexing? top-level.
Simpler use scipy.optimize? Can implement:
  n=len(v)
  u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
  css=np.cumsum(u)
  rng_index=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
  cond = u > (css - 1.0)/rng_index
  if not any: mu=v.max()
  else: mu=(css[cond][-1]-1)/rng_index[-1?] Wait need correct.
Standard: mu = (v[cond].sum() -1)/k. Easier:
  for k? O(n) okay.
  u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
  css=np.cumsum(u)
  idx=np.arange(1,n+1)
  mask=u > (css-1)/idx
  if not np.any(mask): mu=float(u[0])
  else: k=int(np.nonzero(mask)[0][-1]+1); mu=float((css[k-1]-1)/k)
  return np.maximum(v-mu,0)

Pseudo _optimize_p:
best_p, best_score, best_val = p, score, lse
current_val=best_val; current_score=best_score
it=0; fail=0; step=0.1
while time:
  q=conv(p,p); M=max(q); score=2N*M;
  compute weights based on mode/gamma for current q.
  g=grad_square(p,w)
  d=g - mean(g); scale=max(abs(d)); if scale==0 break; d/=scale
  # occasional random kick
  if fail>40: p=project(p + 0.05*rng.normal(0,1)); continue
  best_lse=score? For each factor in [0.25,0.5,1,2,4]:
     cand=project(p - factor*step*d)
     # Optional tiny random noise if fail>10 and factor==1: cand=project(cand + 0.01*rng.normal)*? 
     qc=conv(cand); Mc=max; sc=2N*Mc
     valc = lse(qc, scale) or variance
     accept if (sc < current_score - 1e-12) or (valc < current_val - 1e-8 and sc < best_score + 0.02)
  if accepted: update current, step*=1.1 (cap 0.5)
  else: step*=0.6; fail++; if step<0.005 step=0.1
  if sc < best_score -1e-9: best update
Return best.

Convolution p*p:
def _conv_square(p):
  n=len(p); L=_npow2(2*n-1)
  P=np.fft.rfft(p,n=L)
  C=np.fft.irfft(P*P,n=L)
  return C[:2*n-1]
Since p real; irfft returns length L. Good.

Pseudo code for _optimize_p:
```
def _optimize_p(p, seconds, schedule, rng, label=""):
    N=int(p.size)
    p=np.clip(np.asarray(p,float),0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<=0 return ...; p=p/s
    score=_score_p(p); best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+seconds
    # total time from schedule fractions; process segments
    for mode,param,frac in schedule:
       t=min(frac*seconds, deadline-now); if t<0.2 continue
       if mode=='soft': val = _smooth_value(q, param) else val=score
       step=0.05; fail=0; it=0; last=now
       while now < deadline:
          q=_conv_square(p)
          sc=_score_p(p)
          if sc < best_score: update best
          if mode=='flat': w=q - q.mean(); norm=max(abs(w)); if norm>0 w/=norm else break; val=var
          else: w=_weights_for; val=_smooth_value
          g=_grad_square(p,w)
          draw=g - g.mean()
          dscale=max(abs(draw)); if dscale<=1e-300: break
          d=draw/dscale
          maxp=max(p)
          if fail>40:
             p=project_simplex(p + 0.02*maxp*rng.normal(0,1,N)); fail=10; continue
          best_cand=None; best_valc=val; best_scc=sc
          for fac in (0.3,0.7,1.2,2.5):
             cand=project_simplex(p - fac*step*maxp*d)
             if fail>15 and fac==1.2:
                 cand=project_simplex(cand + 0.01*maxp*rng.normal(0,1,N))
             q2=_conv_square(cand); sc2=_score_p(cand)
             if mode=='flat': val2=float(np.sum((q2-q2.mean())**2))? Use q2 variance scaled.
             else: val2=_smooth_value(q2,param)
             # Choose improvement
             if sc2 < best_scc - 1e-12 or (val2 < best_valc - 1e-9 and sc2 < best_score + 0.05):
                 best_cand=cand; best_valc=val2; best_scc=sc2; best_fac=fac
          if best_cand is not None:
             p=best_cand; sc=best_scc; val=best_valc; step=min(0.25, step*1.1); fail=0
             if sc < best_score: best update
          else:
             step=max(0.005, step*0.6); fail+=1
             if fail>80: # random restart mix
                p=project_simplex(0.5*best_p + 0.5*_random_smooth_p(N,rng,2))
                fail=0; step=0.05
```

Random smooth p:
```
def _random_smooth_p(N, rng, base_kind=0, modes=6, amp_scale=1.0):
    x=np.arange(N,dtype=float)/N
    eps=0.5/N
    if base_kind==0: base=(x+eps)**-0.5
    elif base_kind==1: base=np.ones(N)
    elif base_kind==2: base=(1-x+eps)**-0.5
    else: base=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5,N))
    z=np.zeros(N)
    for k in range(1,modes+1):
       amp=amp_scale/(k*0.7)
       ph=rng.uniform(0,2*math.pi)
       z += amp*np.cos(2*math.pi*k*x + ph)
    z=np.clip(z,-2,2)
    f=base*np.exp(z)
    f=np.maximum(f,1e-6)
    return f/f.sum()
```
Need import math? Use np.pi.

```
def _npow2(m):
    m=int(m)
    if m<=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```

_conv_square:
```
def _conv_square(a):
 a=np.ascont数组; n=a.size
 if n==0: return np.array([])
 L=_npow2(2*n-1)
 A=np.fft.rfft(a,n=L)
 C=np.fft.irfft(A*A,n=L)
 return C[:2*n-1]
```

_score_p:
```
def _score_p(p):
 p=np.asarray(p,float)
 if p.ndim !=1 or p.size==0: return np.inf
 p=np.clip(p,0,None)
 s=float(p.sum())
 if not finite or s<1e-18: return inf
 q=_conv_square(p)
 if q.size==0: return inf
 if np.any(~finite q): return inf
 mc=float(np.max(q))
 if mc<0 or not finite: return inf
 return float(2*p.size*mc/(s*s))
```
For p sum1 scale.

_project_simplex:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
 v=np.asarray(v,float)
 n=v.size
 if n==0: return v
 if n==1: return np.array([1.0])
 u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
 css=np.cumsum(u)
 idx=np.arange(1,n+1,dtype=float)
 mask = u > (css-1.0)/idx
 if not np.any(mask):
    mu=float(u[0])
 else:
    k=int(np.nonzero(mask)[0][-1])+1
    mu=float((css[k-1]-1.0)/k)
 out=np.maximum(v-mu,0.0)
 s=out.sum()
 if s<=0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
 return out/s
```

_weights_soft:
```
def _weights_soft(q, gamma, rng=None):
 L=len(q)
 if L==0: return np.array([])
 M=float(np.max(q))
 if not finite: uniform
 if gamma <= 0: return np.full(L,1/L)
 scale=gamma*max(1,(L-1)//2)
 x=scale*(q-M)
 # clip to [-700,0]
 x=np.clip(x,-700.0,0.0)
 ex=np.exp(x)
 s=ex.sum()
 if s<=0: w=zeros; w[argmax(q)]=1
 else w=ex/s
 return w
```
If q has NaN, return uniform.

_grad_square:
```
def _grad_square(p,w):
 n=len(p); L=len(w)
 if n==0: return p
 size=_npow2(n+L-1)
 P=np.fft.rfft(p,n=size)
 W=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],n=size)
 c=np.fft.irfft(P*W,n=size)
 base=L-1
 g=2.0*c[base:base-n:-1]
 if g.size != n:
    # fallback direct? Use np.convolve for safety if size ok small
    cc=np.convolve(p,w[::-1]); g=2.0*cc[base:base-n:-1]
    if g.size != n: g=np.zeros(n)
 g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0.0)
 return g
```

_make_initial_ps(N, rng, n_random=20):
```
ps=[]
x=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/N; eps=0.5/N
ps.append(_finalize_p(N,(x+eps)**-0.5))
ps.append(_finalize_p(N,(1-x+eps)**-0.5))
for a in (0.4,0.5,0.6,0.8,1.0):
 for b in (0.2,0.5,1.0,1.5):
   f=(x+eps)**(a-1)*(1-x+eps)**(b-1)
   ps.append(_finalize_p(N,f))
# mix sqrt weights
for alpha in (0.2,0.5,0.8):
  f=alpha*(x+eps)**-0.5 + (1-alpha)*(1-x+eps)**-0.5
  ps.append(_finalize_p(N,f))
# shape
for c1 in (-0.6,0.0,0.6):
 for c2 in (0.0,0.8):
  f=(x+eps)**-0.5*(1.0+c1*x+c2*x*x)
  ps.append(_finalize_p(N,f))
# bumps
for center in (0.3,0.5,0.7):
 for sigma in (0.08,0.25):
  for amp in (0.5,1.5):
    f=(x+eps)**-0.5*(1.0+amp*np.exp(-((x-center)**2)/(2*sigma*sigma)))
    ps.append(_finalize_p(N,f))
# random cos
for base_kind in [0,1,2]:
 for _ in range(n_random//3):
   ps.append(_random_smooth_p(N,rng,base_kind))
return ps
```

Previous approach: Given f normalized, solve LP max ∑ g_j subject to g≥0 and (f*g)_k ≤ M for important constraints, rescale g to sum1, update f←(1-t)f+t g. This is a projections/active-set direction. We can approximate: At each step, choose active set of top constraints (or all with q close to max) and solve LP for g. The LP is:
maximize 1^T g
subject to G g ≤ b where G_{k,j} =? (f*g)_k = ∑_j f_j g_{j+k}? Need orientation. For convolution of f and g (same orientation): (f*g)_k =∑_i f_i g_{k-i}. Linear in g. Constraints for active k: ∑_i f_i g_{k-i} ≤ M. g≥0. This LP can be solved, giving a step direction with maximal sum under peak constraints. This may be the "state of the art" approach; implement with scipy.optimize.linprog for N=512/1024 maybe. It could find better local moves than subgradient. Let's add as an option in candidate polishing? Could be heavy but N=512 okay.

LP formulation details: Let f length n, g length n nonnegative. We want (f*g)_k ≤ M for all k active. The full convolution h_k =∑_{i=max(0,k-n+1)}^{min(k,n-1)} f_i g_{k-i}.     res=_scipy_opt.linprog(-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=(0,None), method='highs')

 return best

Let's write _random_smooth_p:
```
def _random_smooth_p(N, rng, base_kind=0):
    x = np.arange(N, dtype=float) / float(N)
    eps = 0.5 / float(N)
    base_kind = int(base_kind)
    if base_kind == 0:
        base = (x + eps) ** (-0.5)
    elif base_kind == 1:
        base = np.ones(N)
    elif base_kind == 2:
        base = (1.0 - x + eps) ** (-0.5)
    else:
        base = np.exp(rng.standard_normal(N) * 0.5)
    z = np.zeros(N, dtype=float)
    for k in range(1, 7):
        amp = 1.0 / (0.7 * k)
        phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + phase)
    z = np.clip(z, -2.0, 2.0)
    f = base * np.exp(z)
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-12)
    s = f.sum()
    if s <= 0: return np.full(N, 1.0/N)
    return f/s
```

_diverse_select manual sort:
```
def _diverse_select(scored, k):
    items=[]
    for sc,p in scored:
       if not np.isfinite(sc): continue
       inserted=False
       for i in range(len(items)):
          if sc < items[i][0]:
             items.insert(i,(sc,p)); inserted=True; break
       if not inserted: items.append((sc,p))
    kept=[]
    for sc,p in items:
       ok=True
       for _,p2 in kept:
          diff=p-p2
          if float(np.dot(diff,diff)) < 0.01: ok=False; break
       if ok:
          kept.append((sc,p))
          if len(kept)>=k: break
    if len(kept)<k:
       for sc,p in items:
          if len(kept)>=k: break
          # if p not already (by id not) use simple: check same L2 <1e-12
          duplicate=False
          for _,p2 in kept:
             if float(np.dot(p-p2,p-p2)) < 1e-24: duplicate=True; break
          if not duplicate: kept.append((sc,p))
    return kept
```
Threshold 0.1 L2. For probability length 512, random difference maybe >0.2. Good.

`_lp_polish` uses _select_active(q, K, rng). Implement:
```
def _select_active(q, K, rng=None):
 L=len(q); K=int(min(L,max(1,K)))
 if rng is None or K>=L: idx=np.arange(L) if K==L else np.argsort(q)[-K:]
 else:
    noise=1e-12*rng.random(L)
    idx=np.argpartition(q+noise, -K)[-K:]
 return idx
```
For deterministic no rng, argsort O(L logL) okay.

`_lp_direction` from active idx:
```
def _lp_direction(f, active_idx, M):
 n=len(f); K=len(active_idx)
 A=np.empty((K,n),float)
 for r,k in enumerate(active_idx):
    lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(k,n-1)
    row=A[r]
    if hi>=lo:
       row[lo:hi+1] = f[k - np.arange(lo,hi+1)]
 b=np.full(K,M)
 c=-np.ones(n)
 bounds=[(0.0,1.0) for _ in range(n)] # This loop top-level okay? It's a list comprehension, fine not lambda.
 res=_scipy_opt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':'highs','dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})? Keep simple.
 if res.status==0: return np.asarray(res.x,float)
 return None
```

Let's ensure _resample_p if p is None. Use:
```
def _resample_p(p,N):
  if p is None: return None
  p=np.asarray(p,float); if p.ndim!=1 or size0: return None
  p=np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return None; p=p/s
  if p.size==N: return p.copy()
  old=(np.arange(p.size)+0.5)/p.size
  new=(np.arange(N)+0.5)/N
  val=np.interp(new, old, p)
  val=np.maximum(val,0); s=val.sum(); if s<=0: val=np.full(N,1/N); else val=val/s
  return val
```

def _sqrt_p(N):

```
def _optimize_p(p, seconds, schedule, rng, label=""):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).copy()
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0); p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return p, np.inf
    p=p/s
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=_score_p(p)
    start=time.monotonic(); deadline=start+max(0.0,float(seconds)); if start+float(seconds) >? no hard.
    total_sec=float(seconds)
    if total_sec <=0: return best_p,best_score
    for mode,param,frac in schedule:
       t_seg=total_sec*float(frac)
       now=time.monotonic(); t_seg=min(t_seg, deadline-now)
       if t_seg <0.3: continue
       seg_deadline=start+ (time? use absolute now+t_seg)
       step=0.1; fail=0; it=0; last_print=time.monotonic()
       if mode == 'flat': val_type='flat'; accept_gamma=0
       else: val_type='smooth'; accept_gamma=80 if (mode!='top' or float(param)>0?)...
       while time.monotonic() < seg_deadline:
          q=_conv_square(p); sc=_score_from_q(p,q)
          if sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_p=p.copy()
          if mode=='flat':
             w=_weights_flat(q)
             if w is None: break
             val=_flat_value(q)
          else:
             if mode=='top': w=_weights_top(q,float(param),rng)
             else: w=_weights_soft(q,float(param),rng)
             val=_smooth_value(q, accept_gamma)
          g=_grad_square(p,w)
          d=g - np.mean(g)
          dmax=float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
          if dmax <= 1e-300:
             break
          d=d/dmax
          pmax=float(np.max(p))
          p_scale=max(10.0/float(p.size), 0.25*pmax)
          if fail>30:
             p=project_simplex(p + 0.05*p_scale*rng.standard_normal(p.size))
             fail=10; continue
          best_cand=None; best_sc=sc; best_val=val
          for fac in (0.5,1.0,2.0):
             cand=project_simplex(p - fac*step*p_scale*d)
             if fail>12 and fac==1.0:
                 cand=project_simplex(cand + 0.01*p_scale*rng.standard_normal(p.size))
             q2=_conv_square(cand); sc2=_score_from_q(cand,q2)
             if mode=='flat': val2=_flat_value(q2)
             else: val2=_smooth_value(q2, accept_gamma)
             if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12 or (val2 < best_val - 1e-10 and sc2 < best_score + 0.1):
                 best_cand=cand; best_sc=sc2; best_val=val2
          if best_cand is not None:
             p=best_cand; sc=best_sc; val=best_val; step=min(0.3, step*1.1); fail=0
             if sc < best_score: best_score=sc; best_p=p.copy(); if now-last_print>10: print...
          else:
             step=max(0.02, step*0.65); fail+=1
             if fail>60:
                p=project_simplex(0.5*best_p + 0.5*_random_smooth_p(p.size,rng,0))
                step=0.1; fail=0
    return best_p,best_score
```

def _weights_top(q,frac,rng=None):

Now, maybe `np.random.default_rng` available? Yes numpy. If not, fallback RandomState? modern.

Let's think about potential memory/time of `_lp_direction` building A for each active: active_idx array length K; in loop `np.arange(lo,hi+1)` per constraint. For K=128,n=512, okay. For 1024,K=192, okay.

Potential linprog upper bound 1.0: If g normalized later, any coordinate >1 clipped. Good.

Now, let's consider if `_lp_polish` improves from uniform? The LP direction with top constraints and bound may produce g. Good.

Could _lp_polish get stuck due M current, if M very small? For p with score target, M~1e-5 for N=8192; but we don't LP large. For N=512 M~3e-3. Linear constraints tight. OK.

Now, final answer code. Need ensure no syntax from type hints? We can include optional? Top-level. Use `list[float]`? Python version maybe 3.9? Avoid type hints for compatibility. No need.

Let's write final code now. Ensure all functions top-level. No lambda. I need avoid comprehensions that use lambda none. List comprehensions are fine no lambda? They include expression, but no lambda function, acceptable. `"[(0.0, 1.0)] * n"` okay.

One more review: In `_weights_top`, `idx = np.argpartition(q + 1e-12 * rng.random(L), -k)[-k:]` if k maybe 1; works. If rng is None, use q. But `rng.random(L)` each top iteration O(L). OK.

In `_select_active` for LP, similar.

Potential issue: In `_lp_direction`, `row[lo:hi + 1] = f[k - np.arange(lo, hi + 1)]`. If hi<lo, skip; row remains zeros. Good. The row values sum? correct.

Potential issue: projection of candidate in LP: If t=0.5, (1-t)*p+t*g, weights sum1. OK.

Potential issue: `_score_from_q` with p not normalized sum !=1. If p from random_cos normalized. Good.

Potential issue: In `_optimize_p`, line search candidates may not have sum exactly1 due projection, but _score_from_q normalizes by s^2, so okay.

Potential issue: In `_optimize_p`, after line search no improve and fail>60, `p = project_simplex(0.5 * best_p + 0.5 * _random_smooth_p(...))`. If best_p sum1 and random sum1, sum1. Good.

Potential issue: For global best updates, if an optimized p at N_mid has score lower, global_p becomes N_mid. Later mid pool may resample global_p to N_mid (same); big pool resample to N_big. OK.

Potential issue: If global_p length smaller than N_mid, _resample_p global to N_mid may improve score and update; but we don't explicitly evaluate after every resample except mid pool. OK.

Let's construct final code. Ensure final answer only code block.
</think>

```python
"""
Search nonnegative step heights by optimizing the equivalent probability law.

For a sequence a with sum S, the score is
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / S^2.
Let p = a / S, so sum(p)=1. Then the score is exactly
    2*n*max(conv(p,p)).
Thus the program optimizes probability vectors p on grids of several lengths,
using projected smooth/subgradient descent, random smooth restarts, analytical
initial families, and a small LP direction step (when scipy is available).
Finally it returns the best probability vector scaled to sum sqrt(2n).
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy import optimize as _scipy_opt
except Exception:
    _scipy_opt = None


def _npow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _p_from_f(f):
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(f.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    f = np.where(np.isfinite(f), f, 0.0)
    f = np.maximum(f, 1e-14)
    s = float(f.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=float)
    return f / s


def _sqrt_p(N):
    N = max(1, int(N))
    x = (np.arange(N, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(N)
    eps = 0.5 / float(N)
    return _p_from_f((x + eps) ** (-0.5))


def _resample_p(p, N):
    if p is None:
        return None
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    p = p / s
    N = max(1, int(N))
    if p.size == N:
        return p.copy()
    old = (np.arange(p.size, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(p.size)
    new = (np.arange(N, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(N)
    val = np.interp(new, old, p)
    val = np.where(np.isfinite(val), val, 0.0)
    val = np.maximum(val, 0.0)
    s = float(val.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N), dtype=float)
    return val / s


def _conv_square(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    L = _npow2(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    C = np.fft.irfft(A * A, n=L)
    return C[:2 * n - 1]


def _score_from_q(p, q):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0 or q.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(p.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    s2 = s * s
    if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        return np.inf
    mc = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(mc):
        return np.inf
    if mc < 0.0:
        mc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / s2)


def _score_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    q = _conv_square(p)
    return _score_from_q(p, q)


def _project_simplex(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(v.size)
    if n == 0:
        return v
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=float)

    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=float)
    mask = u > (css - 1.0) / idx
    if not np.any(mask):
        mu = float(u[0])
    else:
        k = int(np.nonzero(mask)[0][-1]) + 1
        mu = float((css[k - 1] - 1.0) / float(k))
    out = np.maximum(v - mu, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=float)
    return out / s


def _weights_soft(q, gamma, rng=None):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float).ravel()
    L = int(q.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=float)
    gamma = float(gamma)
    if gamma <= 1e-8:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=float)
    M = float(np.max(q))
    scale = gamma * max(1, (L - 1) // 2)
    x = scale * (q - M)
    x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0)
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(ex.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        return w
    return ex / s


def _weights_top(q, frac, rng=None):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float).ravel()
    L = int(q.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=float)
    frac = float(np.clip(frac, 1e-3, 1.0))
    k = max(1, min(L, int(round(frac * float(L)))))
    if k >= L:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / float(L), dtype=float)
    if rng is None:
        idx = np.argpartition(q, -k)[-k:]
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(q + 1e-12 * rng.random(L), -k)[-k:]
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
    return w


def _weights_flat(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float).ravel()
    if q.size == 0:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        return None
    w = q - float(np.mean(q))
    nrm = float(np.max(np.abs(w)))
    if not np.isfinite(nrm) or nrm <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return w / nrm


def _grad_square(p, w):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    L = int(w.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if L == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    size = _npow2(n + L - 1)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, n=size)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], n=size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(P * W, n=size)
    base = L - 1
    g = 2.0 * c[base:base - n:-1]
    if g.size != n:
        c2 = np.convolve(p, w[::-1])
        g = 2.0 * c2[base:base - n:-1]
        if g.size != n:
            g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    return g


def _smooth_value(q, gamma):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float).ravel()
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        return np.inf
    M = float(np.max(q))
    gamma = float(gamma)
    if gamma <= 1e-8:
        return M
    scale = gamma * max(1, (q.size - 1) // 2)
    x = scale * (q - M)
    x = np.clip(x, -700.0, 0.0)
    ex = np.exp(x)
    s = float(ex.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return M
    return M + float(np.log(s) / scale)


def _flat_value(q):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float).ravel()
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
        return np.inf
    return float(np.var(q, dtype=np.float64))


def _random_smooth_p(N, rng, base_kind=0):
    N = max(1, int(N))
    x = np.arange(N, dtype=float) / float(N)
    eps = 0.5 / float(N)
    base_kind = int(base_kind)
    if base_kind == 0:
        base = (x + eps) ** (-0.5)
    elif base_kind == 1:
        base = np.ones(N, dtype=float)
    elif base_kind == 2:
        base = (1.0 - x + eps) ** (-0.5)
    else:
        base = np.exp(0.5 * rng.standard_normal(N))

    z = np.zeros(N, dtype=float)
    for k in range(1, 7):
        amp = 1.0 / (0.7 * float(k))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        z += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(k) * x + ph)
    z = np.clip(z, -2.0, 2.0)
    f = base * np.exp(z)
    return _p_from_f(f)


def _make_initial_ps(N, rng, n_random=24):
    N = max(1, int(N))
    ps = []
    x = (np.arange(N, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(N)
    eps = 0.5 / float(N)

    ps.append(_p_from_f((x + eps) ** (-0.5)))
    ps.append(_p_from_f((1.0 - x + eps) ** (-0.5)))

    for a in (0.40, 0.50, 0.60, 0.80, 1.00):
        for b in (0.20, 0.50, 1.00, 1.50):
            f = (x + eps) ** (a - 1.0) * (1.0 - x + eps) ** (b - 1.0)
            ps.append(_p_from_f(f))

    for alpha in (0.20, 0.50, 0.80):
        f = alpha * (x + eps) ** (-0.5) + (1.0 - alpha) * (1.0 - x + eps) ** (-0.5)
        ps.append(_p_from_f(f))

    for c1 in (-0.60, 0.00, 0.60):
        for c2 in (0.00, 0.80):
            f = (x + eps) ** (-0.5) * (1.0 + c1 * x + c2 * x * x)
            ps.append(_p_from_f(f))

    for center in (0.25, 0.50, 0.75):
        for sigma in (0.08, 0.25):
            for amp in (0.50, 1.50):
                f = (x + eps) ** (-0.5) * (
                    1.0 + amp * np.exp(-((x - center) * (x - center)) / (2.0 * sigma * sigma))
                )
                ps.append(_p_from_f(f))

    base_kinds = [0, 1, 2]
    per_base = max(3, int(n_random) // max(1, len(base_kinds)))
    for bk in base_kinds:
        for __ in range(per_base):
            ps.append(_random_smooth_p(N, rng, bk))

    return ps


def _diverse_select(scored, k):
    items = []
    for sc, p in scored:
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        inserted = False
        for i in range(len(items)):
            if sc < items[i][0]:
                items.insert(i, (sc, p))
                inserted = True
                break
        if not inserted:
            items.append((sc, p))

    kept = []
    for sc, p in items:
        ok = True
        for _, p2 in kept:
            diff = p - p2
            d2 = float(np.dot(diff, diff))
            if d2 < 1e-2:
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            kept.append((sc, p))
            if len(kept) >= int(k):
                break

    if len(kept) < int(k):
        for sc, p in items:
            if len(kept) >= int(k):
                break
            duplicate = False
            for _, p2 in kept:
                diff = p - p2
                if float(np.dot(diff, diff)) < 1e-24:
                    duplicate = True
                    break
            if not duplicate:
                kept.append((sc, p))
    return kept


def _insert_score_p(items, sc, p, max_items):
    if p is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    inserted = False
    for i in range(len(items)):
        if sc < items[i][0]:
            items.insert(i, (sc, p))
            inserted = True
            break
    if not inserted:
        items.append((sc, p))
    while len(items) > int(max_items):
        worst = 0
        for i in range(1, len(items)):
            if items[i][0] > items[worst][0]:
                worst = i
        items.pop(worst)


def _optimize_p(p, seconds, schedule, rng, label=""):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float), np.inf
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=float), np.inf
    p = p / s

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_p(p)
    total_sec = max(0.0, float(seconds))
    if total_sec <= 0.1:
        return best_p, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    max_deadline = start + total_sec

    for mode, param, frac in schedule:
        now = time.monotonic()
        t_seg = min(total_sec * float(frac), max_deadline - now)
        if t_seg <= 0.3:
            continue
        seg_deadline = now + t_seg

        step = 0.10
        fail = 0
        last_print = 0.0
        if mode == 'flat':
            value_kind = 'flat'
            accept_gamma = 0.0
        else:
            value_kind = 'smooth'
            accept_gamma = 80.0

        while time.monotonic() < seg_deadline:
            q = _conv_square(p)
            sc = _score_from_q(p, q)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = p.copy()

            if mode == 'flat':
                w = _weights_flat(q)
                if w is None:
                    break
                val = _flat_value(q)
            elif mode == 'top':
                w = _weights_top(q, float(param), rng)
                val = _smooth_value(q, accept_gamma)
            else:
                w = _weights_soft(q, float(param), rng)
                val = _smooth_value(q, accept_gamma)

            g = _grad_square(p, w)
            d = g - float(np.mean(g))
            dmax = float(np.max(np.abs(d)))
            if not np.isfinite(dmax) or dmax <= 1e-300:
                break
            d = d / dmax

            pmax = float(np.max(p))
            p_scale = max(10.0 / float(n), 0.25 * pmax)
            if p_scale <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(p_scale):
                p_scale = 1.0 / float(n)

            if fail > 30:
                p = _project_simplex(p + 0.05 * p_scale * rng.standard_normal(n))
                fail = 10
                continue

            best_cand = None
            best_sc = sc
            best_val = val

            for fac in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
                cand = _project_simplex(p - fac * step * p_scale * d)
                if fail > 12 and fac == 1.0:
                    cand = _project_simplex(cand + 0.01 * p_scale * rng.standard_normal(n))
                q2 = _conv_square(cand)
                sc2 = _score_from_q(cand, q2)
                if value_kind == 'flat':
                    val2 = _flat_value(q2)
                else:
                    val2 = _smooth_value(q2, accept_gamma)

                if np.isfinite(sc2) and np.isfinite(val2):
                    if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                        best_cand = cand
                        best_sc = sc2
                        best_val = val2
                    elif val2 < best_val - 1e-10 and sc2 < best_score + 0.1:
                        best_cand = cand
                        best_sc = sc2
                        best_val = val2

            if best_cand is not None:
                p = best_cand
                sc = best_sc
                val = best_val
                step = min(0.30, step * 1.10)
                fail = 0
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    nowp = time.monotonic()
                    if nowp - last_print > 8.0:
                        try:
                            print(f"[{label}] n={n} score={best_score:.9f}")
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                        last_print = nowp
            else:
                step = max(0.02, step * 0.65)
                fail += 1
                if fail > 60:
                    p = _project_simplex(0.5 * best_p + 0.5 * _random_smooth_p(n, rng, 0))
                    step = 0.10
                    fail = 0

    return best_p, best_score


def _select_active(q, K, rng=None):
    q = np.asarray(q, dtype=float).ravel()
    L = int(q.size)
    K = max(1, min(L, int(K)))
    if K >= L:
        return np.arange(L)
    if rng is None:
        return np.argsot(q)[-K:] if hasattr(np, 'argsot') else np.argsort(q)[-K:]
    noise = 1e-12 * rng.random(L)
    return np.argpartition(q + noise, -K)[-K:]


def _lp_direction(f, active_idx, M):
    if _scipy_opt is None:
        return None
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(f.size)
    if n == 0:
        return None
    active_idx = np.asarray(active_idx, dtype=int).ravel()
    K = int(active_idx.size)
    if K == 0:
        return None
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    A = np.zeros((K, n), dtype=float)
    for r in range(K):
        k = int(active_idx[r])
        if k < 0 or k > 2 * n - 2:
            continue
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(k, n - 1)
        if hi >= lo:
            A[r, lo:hi + 1] = f[k - np.arange(lo, hi + 1)]

    b = np.full(K, float(M), dtype=float)
    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=float)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    try:
        res = _scipy_opt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds, method="highs")
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = _scipy_opt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=bounds)
        except Exception:
            return None
    if res.status != 0:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float).ravel()
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    return g


def _lp_polish(p, seconds, K, rng, label=""):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return p, np.inf
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(p.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return p, np.inf
    p = p / s

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_p(p)
    if _scipy_opt is None:
        return best_p, best_score

    seconds = max(0.0, float(seconds))
    if seconds <= 0.3:
        return best_p, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + seconds
    tmix = 0.5
    fail = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        q = _conv_square(p)
        sc = _score_from_q(p, q)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_p = p.copy()

        active = _select_active(q, K, rng)
        M = float(np.max(q)) if q.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
            break

        g = _lp_direction(p, active, M)
        if g is None:
            fail += 1
            p = _project_simplex(p + 0.01 * (1.0 / n) * rng.standard_normal(n))
            if fail > 10:
                p = _project_simplex(0.7 * best_p + 0.3 * _random_smooth_p(n, rng, 0))
                tmix = 0.5
                fail = 0
            continue

        gs = float(g.sum(dtype=np.float64))
        if not np.isfinite(gs) or gs <= 1e-18:
            fail += 1
            continue
        g = g / gs
        cand = _project_simplex((1.0 - tmix) * p + tmix * g)
        sc2 = _score_p(cand)

        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < sc - 1e-12:
            p = cand
            sc = sc2
            tmix = min(1.0, tmix * 1.10)
            fail = 0
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = p.copy()
        else:
            tmix = max(0.05, tmix * 0.70)
            fail += 1
            if fail > 14:
                p = _project_simplex(0.7 * best_p + 0.3 * _random_smooth_p(n, rng, 0))
                tmix = 0.5
                fail = 0

    return best_p, best_score


def _transform_p(p, N):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(p.size)
    if n == 0:
        return []
    out = [p.copy()]
    out.append(p[::-1].copy())
    sym = 0.5 * (p + p[::-1])
    out.append(_p_from_f(sym))
    if n > 4:
        x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
        for th in (-0.45, 0.45):
            f = p * (1.0 + th * (x - 0.5))
            out.append(_p_from_f(f))
    return out


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400.0, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    start = time.monotonic()
    allowed = max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    hard_deadline = start + allowed

    if allowed >= 250.0:
        N_big = 8192
        N_mid = 4096
        N_small = 512
        small_frac = 0.18
        mid_frac = 0.35
    elif allowed >= 120.0:
        N_big = 4096
        N_mid = 2048
        N_small = 512
        small_frac = 0.22
        mid_frac = 0.40
    else:
        N_big = 2048
        N_mid = 1024
        N_small = 256
        small_frac = 0.26
        mid_frac = 0.40

    big_frac = 1.0 - small_frac - mid_frac
    if big_frac < 0.25:
        big_frac = 0.25
        mid_frac = max(0.20, mid_frac - 0.05)
        small_frac = max(0.15, small_frac - 0.02)

    small_time = allowed * small_frac
    mid_time = allowed * mid_frac
    big_time = allowed * big_frac

    global_p = _sqrt_p(N_big)
    global_score = _score_p(global_p)
    try:
        print(f"initial fallback length={global_p.size} score={global_score:.9f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    height = None
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
    except Exception:
        h = None
    if h is not None:
        try:
            arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
            arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            s = float(arr.sum(dtype=np.float64))
            if arr.size > 0 and np.isfinite(s) and s > 0.0:
                height = arr / s
                try:
                    print(f"height_sequence_1 length={arr.size}")
                except Exception:
                    pass
        except Exception:
            height = None

    schedule_small = (
        ('soft', 20.0, 0.40),
        ('soft', 100.0, 0.40),
        ('top', 0.20, 0.20),
    )
    schedule_mid = (
        ('soft', 20.0, 0.25),
        ('soft', 100.0, 0.30),
        ('top', 0.05, 0.20),
        ('flat', 0.0, 0.10),
        ('soft', 300.0, 0.15),
    )
    schedule_big = (
        ('soft', 30.0, 0.30),
        ('soft', 200.0, 0.30),
        ('top', 0.02, 0.20),
        ('flat', 0.0, 0.10),
        ('soft', 500.0, 0.10),
    )
    schedule_short = (
        ('soft', 80.0, 0.60),
        ('top', 0.05, 0.40),
    )

    # Optional height candidate at final length.
    if height is not None:
        hp = _resample_p(height, N_big)
        if hp is not None:
            hs = _score_p(hp)
            if np.isfinite(hs) and hs < global_score:
                global_score = hs
                global_p = hp.copy()

    # Small-scale global exploration.
    top_small = []
    if small_time > 5.0:
        init_small = _make_initial_ps(N_small, rng, 18)
        if height is not None:
            hp_s = _resample_p(height, N_small)
            if hp_s is not None:
                init_small.append(hp_s)

        scored = []
        for p in init_small:
            if p is None or p.size != N_small:
                continue
            sc = _score_p(p)
            scored.append((sc, p))
        top_candidates = _diverse_select(scored, min(8, max(1, len(scored))))

        if top_candidates:
            t_each = max(1.0, small_time / float(len(top_candidates)))
            for _, p0 in top_candidates:
                if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 5.0:
                    break
                rem = max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0)
                if rem <= 1.0:
                    break
                t = min(t_each, 0.95 * rem)
                p1, s1 = _optimize_p(p0, t, schedule_small, rng, "small")
                if np.isfinite(s1):
                    _insert_score_p(top_small, s1, p1, 8)
                    if s1 < global_score:
                        global_score = s1
                        global_p = p1.copy()

                if _scipy_opt is not None and N_small <= 1024 and t > 0.5:
                    lp_t = min(1.5, 0.3 * t, max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 5.0))
                    if lp_t > 0.2:
                        p2, s2 = _lp_polish(p1, lp_t, max(32, N_small // 6), rng, "small-lp")
                        if np.isfinite(s2):
                            _insert_score_p(top_small, s2, p2, 8)
                            if s2 < global_score:
                                global_score = s2
                                global_p = p2.copy()

    # Promote best small solutions to mid size.
    if top_small:
        for _, p0 in top_small[:3]:
            pm = _resample_p(p0, N_mid)
            if pm is not None:
                sm = _score_p(pm)
                if np.isfinite(sm) and sm < global_score:
                    global_score = sm
                    global_p = pm.copy()

    # Mid-size polishing.
    scheduled_mid_time = mid_time
    if scheduled_mid_time > 5.0:
        mid_pool = []
        gm = _resample_p(global_p, N_mid) if global_p.size != N_mid else global_p.copy()
        if gm is not None:
            mid_pool.append(gm)
        for _, p0 in top_small[:3]:
            pm = _resample_p(p0, N_mid)
            if pm is not None:
                mid_pool.append(pm)
        if height is not None:
            hm = _resample_p(height, N_mid)
            if hm is not None:
                mid_pool.append(hm)
        for _ in range(3):
            mid_pool.append(_random_smooth_p(N_mid, rng, 0))

        scored_mid = []
        for p in mid_pool:
            if p is None or p.size != N_mid:
                continue
            sc = _score_p(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored_mid.append((sc, p))
        top_mid = _diverse_select(scored_mid, min(3, max(1, len(scored_mid))))
        if top_mid:
            t_each = max(2.0, scheduled_mid_time / float(len(top_mid)))
            for _, p0 in top_mid:
                if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 10.0:
                    break
                rem = max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 10.0)
                if rem <= 2.0:
                    break
                t = min(t_each, 0.95 * rem)
                p1, s1 = _optimize_p(p0, t, schedule_mid, rng, "mid")
                if np.isfinite(s1):
                    if s1 < global_score:
                        global_score = s1
                        global_p = p1.copy()
                    pb = _resample_p(p1, N_big)
                    if pb is not None:
                        sb = _score_p(pb)
                        if np.isfinite(sb) and sb < global_score:
                            global_score = sb
                            global_p = pb.copy()

    # Final-size polishing.
    if big_time > 5.0:
        big_pool = []
        gb = _resample_p(global_p, N_big) if global_p.size != N_big else global_p.copy()
        if gb is not None:
            big_pool.append(gb)
        for _, p0 in top_small[:2]:
            pb = _resample_p(p0, N_big)
            if pb is not None:
                big_pool.append(pb)
        big_pool.append(_sqrt_p(N_big))
        if height is not None:
            hb = _resample_p(height, N_big)
            if hb is not None:
                big_pool.append(hb)

        scored_big = []
        for p in big_pool:
            if p is None or p.size != N_big:
                continue
            sc = _score_p(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored_big.append((sc, p))
        top_big = _diverse_select(scored_big, min(2, max(1, len(scored_big))))
        if top_big:
            t_each = max(2.0, big_time / float(len(top_big)))
            for _, p0 in top_big:
                if time.monotonic() >= hard_deadline - 8.0:
                    break
                rem = max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 8.0)
                if rem <= 2.0:
                    break
                t = min(t_each, 0.98 * rem)
                p1, s1 = _optimize_p(p0, t, schedule_big, rng, "big")
                if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < global_score:
                    global_score = s1
                    global_p = p1.copy()

    # Final lightweight transformations/short polish.
    rem = max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if isinstance(global_p, np.ndarray) and global_p.size > 0 and rem > 4.0:
        best_p = global_p.copy()
        best_sc = global_score
        for tr in _transform_p(best_p, best_p.size):
            if tr is None or tr.size != best_p.size:
                continue
            s = _score_p(tr)
            if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_sc:
                best_sc = s
                best_p = tr.copy()
        if best_sc < global_score:
            global_score = best_sc
            global_p = best_p.copy()

        if rem > 25.0:
            tfin = min(18.0, rem - 8.0)
            if tfin > 5.0:
                pfin, sfin = _optimize_p(best_p, tfin, schedule_short, rng, "final")
                if np.isfinite(sfin) and sfin < global_score:
                    global_score = sfin
                    global_p = pfin.copy()

    # Convert best probability vector to evaluator sequence.
    if global_p is None or np.asarray(global_p).size == 0:
        global_p = _sqrt_p(N_big)

    out_p = np.asarray(global_p, dtype=float).ravel()
    out_p = np.where(np.isfinite(out_p), out_p, 0.0)
    out_p = np.maximum(out_p, 0.0)
    s = float(out_p.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        out_p = _sqrt_p(N_big)
        s = float(out_p.sum(dtype=np.float64))

    target_sum = float(np.sqrt(2.0 * out_p.size))
    a = target_sum * (out_p / s)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)

    try:
        print(f"final length={int(a.size)} estimated_score={_score_p(out_p):.9f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in a]
```
